While regularization that enhances ``global'' Lipschitz continuity was initially considered, it has been limited to ``local'' Lipschitz continuity due to a tradeoff between smoothness and expressiveness. However, it has become apparent that the original implementation is cumbersome and does not provide sufficient smoothing, leading to a preference for simpler implementations. This stems from a discrepancy between theory and implementation, and a more appropriate implementation can expect to facilitate smoothing. Therefore, this paper identifies three reasons why the original implementation does not function adequately and provide remedies for them. This modified regularization performs well across multiple tasks and algorithms, successfully achieving smooth motion while improving control performance. Furthermore, by applying it to sim-to-real reinforcement learning for a quadruped robot, it is demonstrated that smooth motion provides robustness against sudden changes in target velocity commands.

</details>


### [181] [Embedding ISO 10218 Safety Compliance in Robots via Control Barrier Functions for Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13203)
*Federico Parma,Cesare Tonola,Nicola Pedrocchi,Manuel Beschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种基于控制屏障函数（CBF）的预测性安全滤波方法，通过纳入人体加速度数据预测最小人机距离，在满足ISO 10218安全标准的同时，相比传统SSM方法减少63%轨迹误差并避免不必要停机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标准速度与分离监测（SSM）滤波器基于恒定人体速度等保守假设，无法准确预测最小安全距离，导致不必要的操作停机，影响人机协作效率。

Method: 方法I：CBF约束的PD安全滤波器；方法II：任务缩放SQP控制器（施加空间管状约束）。两种方法均将预测性CBF作为不等式约束集成到SQP框架中。

Result: 方法II相比方法I在平均轨迹误差上减少63%，且避免了过度规避机动，在保证ISO 10218合规的同时维持高任务吞吐量。

Conclusion: 所提预测性CBF方法能动态调节执行速度并约束空间偏差，在人机协作安全性-效率权衡中优于传统SSM基线。

Abstract: Human-Robot Collaboration (HRC) requires strict adherence to safety standards, such as ISO 10218, to prevent harmful interactions. Standard Speed and Separation Monitoring (SSM) filters calculate safe robotic speeds based on conservative assumptions, such as constant human velocity, which prevents accurate predictions of minimum separation distances and causes unnecessary operational halts. This paper proposes a Control Barrier Function (CBF) that explicitly incorporates human acceleration data to analytically forward-predict the minimum human-robot separation distance during a worst-case robotic stopping trajectory. To guarantee safety at the control level, this predictive CBF is integrated as an inequality constraint within a Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) framework. Specifically, two methods are proposed: Method I, a CBF-constrained PD safety filter; and Method II, a task-scaling SQP controller that enforces a spatial tube constraint. Simulated and real-world experiments on a UR10e robot evaluate the two proposed methods against a standard industrial SSM module baseline. Results demonstrate that Method II dynamically modulates execution speed and confines spatial deviations. Compared to Method I, Method II achieves a 63\% reduction in mean trajectory error and avoids excessive evasive manoeuvres, ensuring high task throughput while complying with ISO 10218 SSM guidelines.

</details>


### [182] [Proprioceptive-visual correspondence enables self-other distinction in humanoid robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13222)
*Yurun Chen,Tianyuan Gao,Yizhong Ge,Shikun Ban,Yizhou Wang,Hongkai Xiong,Wenjun Zeng,Wentao Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了人形机器人可以通过本体感觉-视觉对应学习自我-他人区分，无需身份标签或运动学模型，并以此构建预测性自我模型，支持下游任务如目标抓取、碰撞规避和动作重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人缺乏区分自我与他人的能力，而这对于社会智能至关重要。现有方法通常依赖身份标签或精确的运动学模型，难以在共享环境中灵活应用。

Method: 利用本体感觉（关节角度）与视觉（摄像头图像）之间的对应关系，让机器人通过自我探索学习区分自我与他人；一旦建立区分，进一步引导出自我模型，将关节配置映射到3D身体占用空间。

Result: 系统在多智能体场景中（包括人类和形态相同的机器人）能可靠识别自身，学习3D自我模型，并成功应用于目标抓取、碰撞感知运动规划和人到机器人运动重定向等下游任务。

Conclusion: 本研究为机器人在共享物理环境中建立身体自我表征提供了一条可行路径，无需预设模型或外部标签，具备良好的泛化性和实用性。

Abstract: Distinguishing self from others is a prerequisite for social intelligence, yet humanoid robots that increasingly share workspaces with humans still lack this ability. Here we show that a humanoid robot can learn self-other distinction from proprioceptive-visual correspondence, without any identity labels or kinematic models. Once established, this distinction bootstraps a predictive self-model that maps joint configurations to three-dimensional body occupancy, capturing how the robot's body changes with action. In multi-agent scenes involving humans or morphologically identical robots, the system reliably identifies itself, learns a 3D self-model, and supports downstream tasks including target reaching, collision-aware motion planning, and human-to-robot motion retargeting. Together, these results outline a route toward bodily self-representation in robots that act and coordinate alongside others in shared physical environments. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/humanoid-self-model/.

</details>


### [183] [WT-UMI: Tactile-based Whole-Body Manipulation via Force-Supervised Contact-Aware Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13232)
*Jaehwi Jang,Zhaoyuan Gu,Alfred Cueva,Zimeng Chai,Junjie Sheng,Thong Nguyen,Himank Galundia,Yifan Wu,Huishu Xue,Isaac Legene,Ojas Mediratta,Davin Doan,Andrew Collins,Sarah Sadegh,KyoungMok Kim,Rishita Dhalbisoi,Zun Chen,Ye Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WT-UMI，一种可穿戴全身触觉接口，支持人类演示和人形遥操作两种模式，通过力条件目标位姿校正、力监督规划器和触觉导纳控制器，在柔性/刚性物体操作和人-人形协作任务中提升了成功率和接触位置跟踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿策略大多隐式处理接触力，无法充分利用不同演示源的互补模态：人类演示提供自然接触力但缺乏机器人可执行动作，遥操作记录机器人动作但力调节不够自然。

Method: 1) 可穿戴全身触觉接口(WT-UMI)，支持人类演示和人形遥操作双模式；2) 力条件目标位姿校正模块，从遥操作数据学习将人体位姿转换为接触感知的机器人目标；3) 力监督规划器，预测末端执行器位姿块和接触力轨迹；4) 基于触觉的导纳控制器，以预测的接触力为参考。

Result: 在五种接触密集任务（可变形物体、大型刚性物体、人-人形协作）中，WT-UMI相比四种基线策略提升了成功率并降低了接触位置跟踪误差。

Conclusion: 结合触觉感知与显式力调节的模仿学习框架能够有效处理接触丰富的操作任务，且双模式设计能兼顾人类演示的自然力交互和遥操作的机器人可执行性。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid manipulation of bulky, deformable, and shared-load objects requires distributed contact sensing and explicit force regulation, yet most imitation policies treat contact force only implicitly. On the other hand, different demonstration sources provide complementary modalities with inherent trade-offs: human demonstrations capture natural contact forces but not robot-executable actions, while teleoperation directly records robot actions but with less natural force regulation. This paper presents \textbf{WT-UMI}, a wearable whole-body tactile interface worn by human operators or mounted on humanoids, providing accurate observations of tactile images, contact forces, and end-effector poses across both human demonstration and humanoid teleoperation modes. We introduce a force-conditioned target-pose correction module that converts measured human poses into contact-aware robot targets by learning corrections from teleoperation data. To leverage the natural force interaction in human data, we propose a force-supervised planner that predicts end-effector pose chunks and contact-force trajectories. The predicted contact force serves as the reference for a tactile-based admittance controller. Across five contact-rich tasks spanning deformable objects, bulky rigid objects, and human--humanoid collaboration, WT-UMI improves success rate and reduces contact-position tracking error over four policy baselines. Our project page is available at https://wt-umi.github.io/WTUMI/.

</details>


### [184] [Humor Style Drives Laughter, Topic Shapes Acceptability: Evaluating Bilingual Personal and Political Robot-Delivered AI Jokes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13256)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Anne-Gwenn Bosser*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探索了在课堂小组环境中，机器人使用大语言模型生成的四种幽默类型（亲和型、自我增强型、攻击型、自嘲型）和两种笑话内容（人物相关 vs. 政治）对人们感知趣味性、适当性及语言偏好的影响。结果表明：幽默类型显著影响趣味性（攻击型和亲和型评分更高），笑话内容主要影响适当性（人物相关笑话优于政治笑话），语言偏好受内容及个体语言流利度和幽默实践影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幽默在人类社交中至关重要，计算幽默的最新进展为人机交互中融入幽默创造了新机会。然而，尽管大语言模型能生成多种形式的幽默，但在小组场景下，幽默风格、笑话内容和语言偏好如何影响人们对机器人呈现幽默的感知，仍不清楚。

Method: 采用混合因子设计，在真实大学教室中让机器人向小组参与者播放AI生成的笑话。参与者评估四种幽默类型（亲和型、自我增强型、攻击型、自嘲型）与两种笑话内容（人物相关 vs. 政治）的趣味性和适当性，同时考察语言偏好。

Result: 幽默类型显著影响趣味性，攻击型和亲和型幽默评分更高；笑话内容主要影响适当性，人物相关笑话比政治笑话更被接受；语言偏好受笑话内容和参与者的语言流利度及幽默实践共同影响。

Conclusion: 在机器人交付幽默时，需谨慎选择幽默类型和内容以确保趣味性和适当性，同时个体语言背景也是影响偏好差异的重要因素。

Abstract: Humor plays a central role in human social relationships, and recent advances in computational humor create new opportunities for integrating humor into human-robot interaction (HRI). While large language models (LLMs) can generate diverse forms of humor, it remains unclear how humor style, joke content, and language preference shape perceptions of robot-delivered humor in group settings. In this exploratory study, we employed a mixed factorial design in which participants evaluated AI-generated jokes delivered by a robot in a university classroom. We examined the effects of humor type (Affiliative, Self-Enhancing, Aggressive, Self-Defeating) and joke content (person-related vs. political) on perceived funniness and appropriateness, as well as preferred language. Results show that humor type significantly influences funniness, with Aggressive and Affiliative humor rated higher, while joke content primarily affects appropriateness, with person-related jokes preferred over political ones. Language preference was shaped by both joke content and participants' self-reported fluency and humor practices.

</details>


### [185] [See Selectively, Act Adaptively: Dual-Level Structural Decomposition for Bimanual Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13279)
*Yoon-Ji Choi,Young-Chae Son,Soo-Chul Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于双层级结构分解的双臂操控VLA框架，通过视图选择性视觉路由和交互感知动作混合专家模块，分别处理视觉相关性和双臂交互结构，在仿真和真实任务中分别比单块基线提升27.7%和43.3%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略通过单一共享表示处理多样视觉输入和双臂交互模式，未能分别考虑视觉相关性和双臂交互结构，导致策略学习困难。

Method: 提出双层级结构分解框架：1) 视图选择性视觉路由(View-Selective Visual Router)，动态调整腕部视角贡献以突出相关视觉线索；2) 交互感知动作混合专家(Interaction-Aware Action MoE)，将动作生成为协调模式和单臂路径，适应不同交互模式。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的6个仿真任务和3个真实世界任务上，相比单块基线，仿真成功率提升27.7%，真实任务提升43.3%，并持续优于单模块变体。

Conclusion: 联合考虑选择性视觉处理和双臂交互结构的显式分解，为鲁棒的双臂操控提供了有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: In bimanual robotic manipulation, task-relevant visual information varies with the task stage and context, while the interaction of the two arms shifts between independent and coordinated modes, making policy learning challenging. However, existing monolithic Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies process diverse visual inputs and interaction patterns through a single shared representation and action generation pathway, often failing to separately account for visual relevance and bimanual interaction structure. To address this issue, we propose a bimanual manipulation VLA framework based on Dual-Level Structural Decomposition. The View-Selective Visual Router dynamically adjusts wrist-view contributions to emphasize relevant visual cues, while the Interaction-Aware Action Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) decomposes action generation into coordinated and arm-wise pathways to adapt to varying bimanual interaction modes. We evaluate the proposed method on six simulated bimanual manipulation tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 and three long-horizon real-world tasks. Our model improves the overall average success rate over a monolithic baseline by 27.7% in simulation and 43.3% in real-world evaluation, while consistently outperforming single-module variants across both settings. These results demonstrate that jointly considering selective visual processing and explicit decomposition of bimanual interaction structures provides an effective inductive bias for robust bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [186] [EMG-Based Adaptation of Anisotropic Virtual Fixtures for Robot-Assisted Surgical Resection and Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13340)
*Dario Onfiani,Michael Dyck,Luigi Biagiotti,Julian Klodmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的自适应各向异性虚拟夹具框架，通过EMG信号实时推断 surgeon 意图并动态调整约束几何，在标准手术训练任务中显著提升了操作精度、运动一致性，同时降低了认知负荷、努力程度和挫败感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统虚拟夹具采用固定几何形状，缺乏适应手术工作流程或外科医生即时意图的灵活性，难以满足精细手术任务（如切除和剥离）的需求。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性虚拟夹具框架，通过EMG信号实时推断外科医生意图，允许外科医生通过前臂肌肉收缩动态扩展或解除约束，实现精准引导运动与工具自由重新定位之间的无缝切换。

Result: 在标准化手术训练任务的试点用户研究中，该方法在任务精度和运动一致性方面取得显著改善，同时降低了感知认知负荷、努力程度和挫败感。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应虚拟夹具框架能有效提升机器人辅助腹腔镜手术的操作性能，具有临床应用的潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the development of an adaptive assistance system for robot-assisted laparoscopic surgery, specifically for delicate tasks such as Resection and Dissection. Even if Virtual Fixtures offer significant advantages for guiding a surgeon's movements, conventional Virtual Fixtures are often defined by fixed geometries, lacking the flexibility to adapt to the surgical workflow or the surgeon's immediate intent. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework for an adaptive and anisotropic virtual fixture. In addition, we introduce an intuitive control interface that modulates the fixture's geometry in real-time based on the surgeon's intent, inferred from EMG signals. This approach allows the surgeon to dynamically expand or disengage the constraint by contracting their forearm muscles, enabling seamless transitions between precise guided motion and free repositioning of the tool. Experimental results from a pilot user study, based on a standardized surgical training task, demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method. The system showed significant improvements in task accuracy and movement consistency, alongside a reduction in perceived cognitive load, effort, and frustration.

</details>


### [187] [Low cost, easily manufactured, highly flexible strain and touch sensitive fiber for robotics applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13352)
*Christian Diaz Herrera,Srushti Raste,Simin Liu,Miles Modeste,Jiyang,Yin,Katelyn McCall,Yuxing Jared Yao,Roopkamal Chahal,Simon Chidley,Trung Ha,T. David Westmoreland,Sonia Roberts*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用低成本商业现成组件（导电缝纫线、硅胶管和环形穿针器）快速制造导电纤维的方法，并验证了其作为电阻式应变传感器和电容式传感器在机器人抓取、姿态估计、触觉传感和近场传感中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人拉伸和触觉传感器在材料成本、制造设备或制造时间方面至少有一项昂贵，亟需一种低成本、快速制造且性能可靠的传感器方案。

Method: 采用导电缝纫线（$0.07/英尺）穿入硅胶管（$0.94/英尺）中，使用环形穿针器（$2）辅助制造，2分钟可制成20厘米长的导电纤维，分别将其用作电阻式应变传感器和电容式传感器。

Result: 电阻式传感方面成功应用于气动辅助手指的抓取触发、气动机器人带的位置感知、柔性固体的姿态估计；电容式传感方面成功应用于触觉触发机械臂运动、近场传感实现机械臂跟随移动手部，并展示了纤维的针织加工性和断纤修复方法。

Conclusion: 该导电纤维制造方法成本极低、速度快、材料易得，兼具应变和触觉传感能力，具有良好的可修复性和可扩展性，为低成本机器人传感提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Existing stretch and touch sensors for robots are generally expensive with respect to at least one of material costs, required manufacturing equipment, or manufacturing time. We present and experimentally characterize a conductive fiber made using only inexpensive commercial off-the-shelf parts (conductive thread at $0.07/ft, silicone tubing at $0.94/ft) and tools (loop-style needle threader at $2), which can be manufactured quickly (20 cm length in 2 minutes.) We demonstrate its use as a resistive strain sensor with three applications: Triggering a grasp in a pneumatically actuated assistive finger, sensing the pose of a pneumatically actuated robotic strap, and estimating the pose of a flexible solid. We also demonstrate that it can be used as a capacitive sensor with two applications: First, as a touch sensor which triggers a commercial robot arm to move, and second, as a near-field sensor enabling the robot arm to follow a moving hand. The capacitive sensors are knitted, showcasing the high flexibility of the fiber. We discuss methods for improving manufacturing scalability and their cost trade-offs. Finally, we demonstrate a method for repairing a cut fiber.

</details>


### [188] [Real-Time Execution with Autoregressive Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13355)
*Sangkyu Lee,Seohyeon Park,Tackgeun You,Avi Caciularu,Idan Szpektor,Hwasup Lim,Youngjae Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明自回归策略通过调整分词范围和约束解码可以实现实时执行，且性能优于同等水平的流匹配策略，在模拟和真实环境中均取得更快的任务完成速度和更好的指令跟随能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉-语言-动作模型在真实部署中需要实时执行（异步推理确保平滑轨迹和快速响应），而现有实时执行工作主要聚焦于扩散策略变体，忽视了自回归策略在同步推理中推理速度较慢的问题。

Method: 通过调整分词范围（tokenization horizon）和采用约束解码（constrained decoding）来保证严格的延迟界限，从而实现多轨迹解码以最大化性能。

Result: 在模拟和真实环境中，自回归策略在任务完成速度上显著优于同等水平的流匹配策略，同时保持更好的指令跟随泛化能力和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 自回归策略通过适当的优化仍可成为支持实时执行的有竞争力的策略类型，挑战了当前对扩散策略在实时执行中主导地位的认知。

Abstract: Real-time execution, enabled by asynchronous inference that ensures both smooth action trajectories and fast reactivity, is critical for realistic deployments of large-scale Vision-Language-Action models. However, recent work on real-time execution primarily focuses on variants of diffusion policies, even though it is more critical for autoregressive policies given their slower rollout speed in synchronous inference. In contrast, we demonstrate that autoregressive policies can achieve real-time execution by adjusting the tokenization horizon and applying constrained decoding, thereby guaranteeing strict latency bounds that enable multi-trajectory decoding to maximize performance. Across simulated and real-world environments, we find that the autoregressive policy consistently outperforms its equivalent-level flow-matching policy counterpart while achieving significantly improved task completion speeds from synchronous inference. Coupled with the inherent advantages of autoregressive policies, such as faster convergence and better generalizability in instruction-following, these results confirm that autoregressive policies can remain a competitive policy type supporting real-time execution.

</details>


### [189] [GeoHAT: Geometry-Adaptive Hybrid Action Transformer for Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13394)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GeoHAT是一个基于扩散策略的端到端全身移动操作框架，通过轻量级傅里叶空间编码器将稠密3D坐标转换为几何token，并采用按token门控融合机制选择性注入视觉基础模型特征；同时提出混合全身动作解码器，将机械臂和基座分解为不同子空间进行解耦控制。在ManiSkill-HAB仿真基准上达到79.3%的平均成功率，超越最强基线23.7%，并在真实世界实验中也取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全身移动操作策略存在以下问题：1）依赖2D特征或稀疏3D表示，缺乏稠密空间结构；2）将机械臂和基座编码在同一动作向量中，忽略了两者不同的控制需求；3）现有稠密融合策略在噪声深度下会破坏预训练表示，且计算开销大。因此需要一种既能有效融合几何信息又不破坏语义先验、且能解耦控制全身动作的框架。

Method: GeoHAT包含三个核心组件：1）轻量级傅里叶空间编码器，将逐像素稠密3D坐标映射为几何token，无需额外3D视觉骨干网络；2）基于深度有效性的逐token门控融合机制，将几何token选择性注入视觉基础模型特征中；3）混合全身动作解码器，将机械臂和基座分解为独立子空间，通过稀疏交叉注意力让各动作模态关注其任务相关的视觉上下文，并使用时序因果建模捕捉帧内协调与帧间依赖。

Result: 在ManiSkill-HAB仿真基准上，GeoHAT平均成功率达79.3%，超越最强基线23.7%。真实世界实验在多种任务上也一致优于所有基线方法。

Conclusion: "几何应仅在可靠处注入、仅在需要处关注"这一简单原则能有效指导全身移动操作框架设计。通过选择性几何融合与解耦动作解码，GeoHAT在仿真和真实场景中均显著提升了操作性能。

Abstract: Whole-body mobile manipulation requires coordinating mobile base and manipulator under shifting viewpoints, posing challenges in geometric perception and action generation. Current policies either rely on 2D features or sparse 3D representations that lack dense spatial structure, and typically encode arm and base within one action vector that ignores their distinct control demands. Moreover, existing dense fusion strategies risk corrupting pretrained representations under noisy depth while incurring heavy computational overhead. We present GeoHAT, an end-to-end diffusion-based framework built on a simple principle: geometry should be injected only where reliable and attended to only where needed. GeoHAT employs a lightweight Fourier spatial encoder that maps dense per-pixel 3D coordinates into geometric tokens without an additional 3D vision backbone. These tokens are then selectively injected into vision foundation model features through per-token gated fusion modulated by depth validity, preserving the semantic prior while enriching spatial understanding. For action generation, a Hybrid Whole-Body Action Decoder decomposes arm and base into distinct subspaces and lets each action modality attend to its task-relevant visual context through sparse cross-attention, while causal temporal modeling captures intra-timestep coordination and inter-timestep dependencies. Experiments on the ManiSkill-HAB simulation benchmark demonstrate that GeoHAT achieves a 79.3% mean success rate, surpassing the strongest baseline by 23.7%. Furthermore, real-world experiments on diverse tasks also confirm consistent improvements over all baselines.

</details>


### [190] [GIVE: Grounding Human Gestures in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13435)
*Pengfei Liu,Gen Li,Junqiao Fan,Boyu Ma,Jindou Jia,Yang Xiao,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GIVE通过视觉和语义双路径增强预训练VLA模型的手势理解能力，在无需修改架构的情况下，将目标识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型将机器人操作视为纯文本驱动任务，忽略了手势在人机交互中的重要作用，导致语言指令模糊时意图定位不准确、操作不可靠。

Method: 提出GIVE方法，通过两条互补路径增强VLA模型：视觉路径将手部骨架和指尖射线叠加到机器人观测中实现显式目标定位；语义路径生成手势的高级描述和任务指令，实现鲁棒的意图定位。

Result: 真实世界HRI实验中，目标物体识别准确率提升40%，任务成功率提升80%，且对未见过的空间布局和不同参与者具有强鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: GIVE有效证明了将手势信息通过视觉和语义双路径注入预训练VLA模型，能在不修改架构的前提下显著提升人机交互中模糊指令下的操纵性能。

Abstract: Human communication is inherently multimodal, where language is often accompanied by non-verbal cues such as gestures to convey intentions. However, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models treat robotic manipulation as a pure text-driven task, overlooking the important role of gestures in Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). This often leads to inaccurate intent grounding and unreliable manipulation when language instructions are ambiguous or underspecified. To address this challenge, we propose GIVE (Gesture Intent via Visual-Semantic Enhancement), an effective approach that enhances pre-trained VLA models with human gesture understanding without architectural modifications. Specifically, GIVE incorporates gesture information through two complementary pathways: a visual pathway that overlays hand skeletons and fingertip rays onto robot observations for explicit object grounding, and a semantic pathway that generates high-level descriptions of human gestures and task instructions for robust intent grounding. By jointly leveraging visual and semantic guidance, GIVE enables VLA policies to better associate gestures with manipulation behaviors and adapt to dynamic interaction intents. In real-world HRI experiments, GIVE substantially outperforms the baseline, improving target object recognition accuracy by 40% and overall task success rate by 80%, while demonstrating strong robustness and generalization to unseen spatial layouts and diverse participants.

</details>


### [191] [NavWAM: A Navigation World Action Model for Goal-Conditioned Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13494)
*Daichi Azuma,Taiki Miyanishi,Koya Sakamoto,Shuhei Kurita,Yaonan Zhu,Petr Khrapchenkov,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NavWAM，一种扩散Transformer策略，通过将未来观测、目标进度值和动作块编码到共享潜序列中，将导航世界模型预测直接转化为可执行动作，在真实机器人部署中优于基于规划的世界模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的导航世界模型仅作为预测模块，需要外部规划器将预测的未来转化为闭环控制，缺乏将视觉预测直接映射为可执行动作的端到端能力。

Method: NavWAM采用扩散Transformer架构，在共享潜序列中联合学习未来观测预测、目标进度值估计和动作块生成，通过仿真预训练和真实机器人适配实现闭环控制。

Result: 在离线基准测试和真实机器人闭环部署中，NavWAM在默认策略模式下（无需CEM风格动作搜索）优于基于规划的世界模型基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合学习预测与行为目标，NavWAM使视觉预测可直接用于机器人控制，为导航世界模型提供了更实用的闭环策略范式。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned visual navigation requires a robot to act under partial observability by anticipating how its motion will change the future egocentric view and whether that change brings it closer to the goal. Navigation world models provide such visual foresight, but they remain prediction modules that require an external planner to convert predicted futures into closed-loop control. We propose Navigation World Action Model (NavWAM), a diffusion-transformer policy that turns navigation world-model prediction into executable action by representing future observations, goal-progress values, and action chunks in a shared latent sequence. By learning future prediction jointly with the action and value targets that determine closed-loop behavior, NavWAM makes visual foresight directly usable for robot control. We build NavWAM through simulation pretraining and real-robot adaptation, and evaluate it on image-goal navigation against planning-based world models and a representative direct navigation policy. Across offline benchmarks and closed-loop real-robot deployment, NavWAM improves over planning-based world-model baselines in our evaluations while using the default policy mode without CEM-style action search. Project page: https://dachii-azm.github.io/navwam/

</details>


### [192] [SPARC: Reliable Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13497)
*Nils Blank,Paul Mattes,Maximilian Xiling Li,Jakub Suliga,Thomas Roth,Moritz Reuss,Pankhuri Vanjani,Rudolf Lioutikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SPARC是一种风险感知框架，利用机器人任务固有的时空结构自动标注演示数据并生成可靠性评分，显著优于纯检测基线，在保留更多样本的同时减少噪声标签，并在目标定位、空间推理及真实场景策略中取得领先结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动标注管线虽能大规模生成结构化空间标注（如边界框、轨迹、操作阶段标签），但缺乏可靠的质量信号——检测器置信度与标注正确性校准不良，导致用户不得不在接受噪声标签或丢弃有用样本之间做出选择。

Method: SPARC利用机器人任务固有的时空结构生成可靠性信号，对每个标注赋予可靠性分数，从而在减少噪声标签的同时保留更多有用样本。此外，还引入了IA-Bench基准用于评估模型在机器人演示中定位交互物体位置的能力。

Result: 在涵盖多种本体和场景的1.7k人工标注演示上，SPARC在定位精度上显著优于纯检测基线，在高精度操作点下保留的样本数量是基线的三倍。基于SPARC标注微调的模型在物体定位和指向基准上达到同类模型最佳，在更广泛的空间推理套件上保持竞争力，且无需人工验证或标注训练数据。在视觉混乱、模糊的真实场景中，基于SPARC标注训练的策略优于基线。

Conclusion: SPARC通过可靠性校准的结构化空间标注框架，有效解决了自动标注缺乏质量信号的问题，验证了时空结构信息在提升标注质量和下游任务性能方面的价值。

Abstract: This work introduces Spatial Annotations from Robot Demonstrations with Reliability Calibration (SPARC), a risk-aware framework that automatically labels robot demonstrations with structured spatial annotations and assigns each annotation a reliability score. Structured spatial annotations, such as bounding boxes, object trajectories, and manipulation phase labels, benefit a broad range of robotics applications from training grounded robot policies and embodied foundation models to motion planning and hierarchical task composition. Existing automated pipelines generate such annotations at scale but provide no reliable quality signal: detector confidence is poorly calibrated for annotation correctness, forcing a choice between accepting noisy labels or discarding useful samples. In contrast to existing automated pipelines, SPARC leverages the spatio-temporal structure inherent to robot tasks to generate a reliability signal, reducing noisy labels and retaining more useful samples. We further introduce Interaction-Aware Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark that measures model accuracy in grounding the locations of interacted objects in robot demonstrations. On 1.7k human-annotated demonstrations spanning diverse embodiments and scenarios, SPARC significantly outperforms detection-only baselines in localization accuracy while retaining three times more samples at high-precision operating points. Our experiments demonstrate that models finetuned on our annotations achieve state-of-the-art results on object-grounding and pointing benchmarks among similarly sized models, while remaining competitive on broader spatial-reasoning suites without manually verified or annotated training data. Furthermore, policies trained on SPARC-generated annotations outperform baselines in cluttered, visually ambiguous real-world scenes. Code, data, and models are available at intuitive-robots.github.io/sparc-labeling.

</details>


### [193] [$\texttt{WEAVER}$, Better, Faster, Longer: An Effective World Model for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13672)
*Arnav Kumar Jain,Yilin Wu,Jesse Farebrother,Gokul Swamy,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WEAVER，一种多视图世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失预测未来潜变量和奖励值，同时在保真度、一致性和效率三个维度上达到最优，在机器人操作任务中实现了策略评估（ρ=0.870）、策略改进（成功率提升38%）和测试时规划（成功率提升14%，速度提升5-10倍）的显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人领域的潜力巨大，但现有方法无法同时满足三个关键需求：高保真度（模拟轨迹与真实世界相关）、长程一致性（长时间跨度内轨迹连贯）和计算效率（快速生成模拟轨迹）。

Method: WEAVER采用多视图世界模型架构，通过流匹配损失（flow-matching loss）训练来预测未来潜在状态和奖励值。架构设计的关键决策包括模型架构、记忆机制和预测目标的设计。

Result: 在机器人硬件上验证：策略评估与真实世界成功率的相关系数ρ=0.870；在π₀.₅机器人基础模型基础上，策略改进使真实世界成功率提升38%；测试时规划使真实世界成功率提升14%，且相比此前世界模型速度提升5-10倍。在分布外场景中也优于此前模型。

Conclusion: WEAVER通过创新的多视图世界模型架构，首次同时实现了世界模型的保真度、一致性和效率三个关键需求，为机器人操作任务中的策略评估、改进和规划提供了有效的模拟基础。

Abstract: The potential impacts of world models (WMs, i.e., learned simulators) on robotics are far-reaching -- policy evaluation, policy improvement, and test-time planning -- all with limited real-world interaction. To unlock these downstream capabilities, a WM needs to jointly satisfy three desiderata: $\textit{(i)}$ fidelity (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that correlate with reality), $\textit{(ii)}$ consistency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories that are coherent over long horizons), and $\textit{(iii)}$ efficiency (i.e., producing simulated trajectories quickly). We propose $\texttt{WEAVER}$ (World Estimation Across Views for Embodied Reasoning): a WM architecture that simultaneously achieves all three desiderata, providing state-of-the-art results on robotic manipulation tasks. $\texttt{WEAVER}$ is a multi-view WM trained to predict future latents and reward values via a flow-matching loss. We distill the key design decisions across model architecture, memory, and prediction objectives required to unlock the kinds of long-horizon dynamic manipulation tasks that have confounded prior world modeling approaches. We apply $\texttt{WEAVER}$ in robotic hardware, demonstrating its effectiveness at policy evaluation ($ρ$=0.870 correlation with real-world success rate), policy improvement (real-world success rate improvement of $38\%$ on top of the $π_{0.5}$ robot foundation model), and test-time planning (real-world success rate improvement of $14\%$ with a $5-10\times$ speedup over prior WMs). $\texttt{WEAVER}$ also demonstrates better performance than prior WMs when evaluated on out-of-distribution scenarios. Code, models, and videos at: https://arnavkj1995.github.io/WEAVER/ .

</details>


### [194] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，通过逆向流策略找到次优动作的潜在噪声并将其映射到最优动作模式，从而提升通用策略在零样本控制、行为克隆蒸馏和强化学习引导方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用策略虽能学习广泛技能，但在面对新任务时，如何从策略丰富的先验行为中推断并调用适当行动仍然困难，尤其是当直接命令策略失败时。

Method: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)：将次优但"合理"的动作通过流策略逆向传递找到其潜在噪声，然后将这些噪声映射到附近通用策略的最优动作模式上。

Result: 在多个仿真和真实操作场景中，FRS能够：1) 将人类或VLM的粗粒度语义指导转化为好的机器人动作，提升零样本控制；2) 通过行为克隆蒸馏训练辅助策略输出噪声，在不到一分钟训练内实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；3) 通过强化学习引导实现策略提升，在标准RL无法改进的任务上取得进步。

Conclusion: FRS是一种有效的通用策略增强方法，能够利用次优动作通过逆向流和重映射来提升通用策略在新任务上的表现，并支持知识蒸馏和强化学习引导。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging news tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>


### [195] [Mana: Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13677)
*Zhao-Heng Yin,Guanya Shi,Pieter Abbeel,C. Karen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Mana框架，将灵巧操作重新解释为动画问题，通过从粗到细的流水线自动生成抓取关键帧和操作轨迹，实现铰接工具的零样本仿真到现实迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接工具操作因其内部自由度协调和接触丰富交互的复杂性而未被充分探索，需要一种可扩展的通用框架。

Method: 借鉴计算机动画的从粗到细流水线：通过程序化生成抓取关键帧（仅需少量鼠标点击指定功能可供性），再结合运动规划和强化学习生成操作轨迹，实现自动化的数据生成。

Result: 在四种不同尺度和关节类型的铰接工具上，实现了抓取和手内操作的零样本仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: Mana提供了一种可扩展的灵巧铰接工具操作方法，证明了将操作重新定义为动画问题的有效性。

Abstract: Articulated tool manipulation remains a major challenge in dexterous robotics due to the need to coordinate internal degrees of freedom and contact-rich interactions. While prior work has largely focused on rigid objects, articulated tool use remains underexplored because of its physical complexity and the difficulty of learning functional grasping and manipulation policies. We present Mana (Manipulation Animator), a general sim-to-real framework that reinterprets dexterous manipulation as an animation problem. Inspired by computer animation, Mana employs a coarse-to-fine pipeline that transforms procedurally-generated grasp keyframes into manipulation trajectories through motion planning and reinforcement learning. The data generation process is largely automatic, requiring only a few mouse clicks to specify functional affordances (<1 minute per tool). Across four articulated tools spanning different scales and joint types, Mana achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer for both grasping and in-hand manipulation, demonstrating a scalable approach to dexterous articulated tool use.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Mixed-Categorical Black-Box Optimization via Information-Geometric Bilevel Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12885)
*Marc Ong,Shinichi Shirakawa,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 针对混合类别-连续变量黑箱优化中变量间强交互导致性能下降的问题，提出一种双层优化框架，在外层优化类别变量、内层优化连续变量，并引入热身启动策略降低计算成本，在二元-连续域上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CMA-ES衍生方法假设类别变量与连续变量相互独立，在面对强交互作用时性能显著下降，需要一种能显式建模此类交互的优化方法。

Method: 提出双层优化框架：外层对类别变量进行随机松弛优化（信息几何优化），内层在每个类别配置下对连续变量进行条件优化；引入热身启动策略，通过缓存和选择最佳配置加速内层搜索。

Result: 在二元-连续域基准测试中，所提方法在交互处理能力上超越现有最优方法，同时计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 双层优化框架能有效捕获类别与连续变量间的相互作用，且通过热身启动策略缓解了双层优化固有的高计算成本问题。

Abstract: Mixed categorical-continuous optimization arises in many practical domains, yet remains challenging. In the black-box setting, evolution strategy-based approaches have shown promise in extending the efficiency and robustness of the CMA-ES to mixed-variable spaces. However, these methods exhibit worsened performance when strong categorical-continuous interactions are present, as their underlying search distributions assume independence between categorical and continuous variables. To address this limitation, we propose a bilevel optimization framework that explicitly captures such interactions by optimizing over categorical variables in an outer loop, and over continuous variables conditioned on each categorical configuration in an inner loop. We formulate each level of the bilevel problem as a stochastic relaxation under information-geometric optimization. To mitigate the high computational cost inherent to bilevel optimization, we introduce a warm-starting strategy that accelerates the lower-level search by selecting the best among multiple cached configurations and updating the cache after each iteration. Experimental results on binary-continuous domain demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing state-of-the-art approaches in interaction-handling capability while also being more computationally efficient across benchmarks encompassing both previously reported and newly proposed types of interaction.

</details>


### [197] [Multi-Objective Coevolution of Prompts and Templates for Circuit Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13089)
*Martin Tomasovic,Lukas Sekanina*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用现成大语言模型（无需领域特定训练）的协同进化算法，自动设计优化的8位近似乘法器，在错误-面积权衡上优于EvoApproxLib库中的高度优化电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似乘法器通过牺牲计算精度来换取功耗、延迟和面积效率的提升，适用于神经网络等容错应用。现有设计方法需要领域专业知识，作者希望利用LLM的能力自动化这一设计过程。

Method: 采用协同进化算法，同时进化一个候选电路群体和一个提示模板群体，提示模板用于引导LLM驱动的电路修改。使用现成LLM（无需领域特定训练）来生成和优化电路设计。

Result: 针对多个目标设计目标的实验表明，该方法发现的近似乘法器在错误-面积权衡方面优于EvoApproxLib库中高度优化的电路。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的协同进化方法无需领域特定训练即可自动生成高性能近似乘法器，在错误-面积权衡上取得更优结果。

Abstract: Approximate multipliers deliberately relax computational accuracy to achieve gains in power efficiency, latency, and silicon area, which makes them well-suited for error-resilient applications such as neural networks. In this work, we introduce a co-evolutionary algorithm that leverages an off-the-shelf large language model (LLM) without requiring domain-specific training to automate the design of optimized 8-bit approximate multipliers. The approach simultaneously evolves a population of candidate circuits and a population of prompt templates that steer LLM-driven modifications. Experimental results for several target design objectives demonstrate that the proposed method discovers approximate multipliers with improved error-area trade-offs compared to highly optimized circuits from the EvoApproxLib library.

</details>


### [198] [Runtime Analysis of the $(μ+ 1)$-ES in a Homogenous Progress Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13323)
*Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于研究进化策略(ES)适应度进展的简化模型，通过假设突变后子代相对于父代的适应度服从不变分布Z来绕过底层适应度景观。作者严格分析了连续稳态(μ+1)-ES在该模型中的期望增长率R_μ，通过构造改进过程来夹逼原始过程，并在Z服从正态分布且μ≤e^δ的条件下给出了R_μ的紧界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略在通用问题中的适应度进展分析通常因底层适应度景观复杂而难以处理。作者旨在提出一种简化模型，绕开具体的适应度景观，用不变分布Z描述突变后的相对适应度变化，从而在远离全局最优的场景下近似优化过程，适用于超参数调优等难以精确建模适应度函数的任务。

Method: 引入了不变分布Z描述突变后子代相对于父代的适应度。针对具有世代重叠的连续稳态(μ+1)-ES，构造改进过程来夹逼原始过程，通过分析改进过程的增长率来获得原始过程期望增长率R_μ的紧界。

Result: 当Z服从正态分布N(-δ,1)且μ≤e^δ时，期望增长率满足R_μ = (log^{1+o(1)} μ)/μ * R_1。

Conclusion: 该简化模型能有效近似进化策略在远离全局最优时的优化过程，且对(μ+1)-ES的严格分析揭示了增长率与种群规模μ及分布参数δ之间的定量关系。

Abstract: We introduce a new simple model to study the fitness progress of Evolution Strategies (ES) in generic problems. In this model, we bypass the underlying fitness landscape and assume that the mutation of any individual produces an offspring whose fitness relative to the parent is given by an invariant distribution $Z$, such as a mean-shifted Gaussian. This serves as a prototypical model for the optimisation landscape when an evolution algorithm operates far from the global optimum. This simple model can be used to approximate the optimisation process for problems where it is intractable to model the exact fitness function, including tasks such as hyperparameter tuning in machine learning models.
  We rigorously analyse the expected growth rate $\mathcal{R}_μ$ of the continuous steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES in this model. Unlike comma-selection strategies, the steady-state $(μ+1)$-ES maintains overlapping generations, introducing complex mathematical dependencies among surviving parents that make it harder to analyse. We give a general technique to analyse the the $(μ+ 1)$-ES by constructing modified processes whose growth rates provably sandwich that of the original process. These modified processes are then easier to analyse but still close enough to the true process to give a tight bound on the expected growth rate. When $Z = \mathcal{N}(-δ, 1)$ and $μ\le e^δ$, we show that $\mathcal{R}_μ = \frac{\log^{1 + o(1)} μ}μ \mathcal{R}_1$.

</details>


### [199] [Improved Runtime Bound for the $(μ+ 1)$ EA on BinVal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13344)
*Joris Belder,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 研究证明了当μ = o(n/log n)时，(μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数上的上界为O(μ log μ · n log n)，显著改进了之前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的结果，且该界表明(μ+1) EA在BinVal上的性能比OneMax最多慢O(log μ · log n)倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进(μ+1) EA在Binary Value函数（BinVal）上的运行时间上界，缩小其与OneMax问题上的性能差距。

Method: 通过理论分析，对多种变异算子（包括标准位变异）下的(μ+1) EA在BinVal问题上的运行时间进行上界推导。

Result: 得到了O(μ log μ · n log n)的上界（当μ = o(n/log n)时），优于之前O(μ^5 n log(n/μ^4))的结果。

Conclusion: (μ+1) EA在BinVal上的性能与OneMax相比，最多只慢O(log μ · log n)倍，表明该算法在两类函数上的表现差距不大。

Abstract: We study the $(μ+1)$ EA on the Binary Value function BinVal. We show that it needs at most $O(μ\log μ\cdot n \log n)$ function evaluations to find the optimum when $μ= o(n/\log n)$. This substantially improves upon the recent upper bound of $O(μ^5 n \log(n/μ^4))$ by Krejca, Neumann and Witt. Our results hold for several mutation operators including standard bit mutation. In particular, our bound implies that the $(μ+1)$ EA is at most a factor $O(\log μ\cdot \log n)$ slower on BinVal than on OneMax.

</details>


### [200] [The $(1 + 1)$-EA in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13360)
*Georg Hasebe,Johannes Lengler,Raghu Raman Ravi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文研究了动态线性环境下(1+1)-EA的优化行为，发现突变率参数χ存在尖锐阈值：低于阈值时优化时间为O(n log n)，高于阈值时变为2^Ω(n)。对于动态二进制值问题，还发现了第二阈值，量化了优化停滞的距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解进化算法在动态环境（每代重新采样目标函数）中的性能，特别是突变率对算法效率的影响。

Method: 理论分析(1+1)-EA在两种动态线性函数模型（Dynamic Binary Value和Uniform weight）下的运行时间，证明突变率参数χ的阈值效应。

Result: 发现了突变率χ的尖锐阈值：低于阈值时优化时间为O(n log n)，高于阈值时变为指数级2^Ω(n)。对于Dynamic Binary Value问题，还发现了第二阈值量化了优化停滞的距离。

Conclusion: 动态线性环境下(1+1)-EA的突变率存在临界阈值，该阈值决定了算法效率的质变；同时存在第二阈值决定可达精度，验证了先前的实证发现。

Abstract: We study the $(1 + 1)$-EA in dynamic linear environments, where in every generation selection is performed with respect to a freshly sampled linear function with positive weights. We consider the Dynamic Binary Value problem, where each generation uses a uniformly random permutation of $1,2,4,\dots,2^{n-1}$, and a Uniform weight variant, where the weights are drawn independently from $\mathrm{Unif}(0,1)$. Both of them have recently been integrated into the IOHprofiler platform and empirically studied.
  For both models we prove a sharp threshold in the mutation parameter $χ$ for mutation rate $χ/n$. Below the threshold, the expected optimisation time is $\mathcal{O}(n\log n)$, whereas above it the runtime becomes $2^{Ω(n)}$.
  For the Dynamic Binary Value problem in the exponential regime, we also quantify at what distance from the optimum the optimisation process stagnates. We show that there is a second threshold: a distance that is efficiently reached, but reaching any smaller distance takes exponential time. This quantifies and proves previous empirical findings.

</details>


### [201] [Adaptive-Frequency Resonate-and-Fire Neurons for Spectral Estimation of Streaming Radar Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13516)
*Stefano Chiavazza,Sen Yuan,Marc Geilen,Francesco Fioranelli,Federico Corradi*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自适应谐振-放电（ARF）神经元的类脑信号处理方法，以逐样本方式处理FMCW雷达信号，实现目标距离-速度估计，内存消耗仅与目标数量成正比，无需存储整个数据块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FFT方法需要存储和处理大量数据块，在内存受限或低延迟场景下成为瓶颈。本文旨在提出一种内存高效、可逐样本处理的替代方案。

Method: 将ARF神经元建模为离散时间动力系统，每个神经元动态调整其内部频率以匹配输入雷达信号的主要频率分量；引入反馈机制使多个神经元锁定不同频率分量，实现多目标追踪。

Result: 仿真和实验数据表明该方法能成功跟踪多个目标；相比FFT方法，内存使用降至仅与目标数量成正比。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于资源受限和边缘计算场景下的雷达应用，为类脑计算与雷达信号处理的交叉提供了新思路。

Abstract: Frequency Modulated Continuous Wave (FMCW) radar systems traditionally rely on Fourier-based methods, such as the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT), to estimate target range and velocity. While computationally efficient, these approaches require storing and processing large blocks of data, which can become a bottleneck in memory-constrained or low-latency applications. In this work, we propose a neuromorphic-inspired signal processing method based on adaptive resonate-and-fire (ARF) neurons formulated as a discrete-time dynamical system. Each neuron dynamically adjusts its internal frequency to match dominant frequency components of the input radar signal, enabling direct estimation of target ranges and velocities without computing the full frequency spectrum. The proposed model operates in a sample-by-sample fashion, resulting in memory requirements that scale with the number of tracked targets rather than the signal length. A feedback mechanism is also introduced to enable multiple neurons to lock on distinct frequency components in multi-target cases. Results on simulated and experimental data demonstrate that the method can successfully track multiple targets. Compared to conventional FFT-based approaches, the proposed method offers reduced memory usage proportional only to the number of tracked targets, making it suitable for resource-constrained and edge-based radar applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.PL'></div>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [nomp: A Framework for Building Domain Specific Compilers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12650)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Kaushik Kulkarni,Nipuna Fernando,Pubudu Hewavitharana,Hirumal Priyashan,Poorna Gunathilaka,Nagitha Abeywickrema,Ravindu Hirimuthugoda,Tarun Prabhu,Kirshanthan Sundararajah,Sanath Jayasena*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出nomp框架，通过基于pragma的编程模型和运行时系统，在领域特定编译器中复用优化模式，旨在在不牺牲性能和可移植性的前提下提高程序员生产力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低层GPU编程模型（CUDA、HIP等）性能优异但学习曲线陡峭，高层模型（OpenMP、OpenACC等）提高生产力却难以达到同等性能。现有方法在生产力、可移植性和性能三者间存在固有权衡，缺乏同时兼顾三者的通用解决方案。

Method: 提出nomp框架，包含基于pragma的编程模型和运行时系统，支持根据用户提供的元数据进行代码转换和生成，通过复用特定领域优化模式来弥合高层抽象与底层性能之间的差距。

Result: nomp能在保持与低层模型相当性能的同时，通过领域特定优化模式的复用提升程序员生产力，并维持良好的可移植性。

Conclusion: 通过领域特定编译器框架nomp，可以在不牺牲性能和可移植性的前提下显著提升程序员生产力，为GPU编程中生产力-性能-可移植性的权衡问题提供了一种可行的折中方案。

Abstract: The low-level GPU programming models (CUDA, HIP, OpenCL, etc.) provide detailed control of the data flow and execution plan of a program in order to extract close-to-metal performance. However, these have a steep learning curve due to the intricacies of their syntax and semantics. This reduces programmer productivity. On the other hand, high-level models (OpenMP, OpenACC, etc.) that serve as abstractions over the low-level models are aimed at improving programmer productivity but achieving performance on-par with the low-level models is a challenge. There are inherent trade-offs between productivity, portability and performance in both approaches and there is no one-size-fits-all solution which achieves all three simultaneously. However, we believe there is room to improve programmer productivity without sacrificing performance and portability by reusing optimization patterns specific to a given domain. To this end, we propose nomp: a framework for building domain specific compilers. nomp consists of a pragma based programming model and a runtime capable of code transformation and generation based on user provided metadata.

</details>


### [203] [Functional Cache Grafting: Robust and Rapid Code-Policy Synthesis for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13097)
*Saehun Chun,Wonje Choi,Sera Choi,Sanghyun Ahn,Honguk Woo*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: FCGraft通过维护函数级验证代码骨架及其KV缓存，采用拼接和修补两种缓存嫁接策略，为具身Agent生成可执行代码策略，相比RAGCache实现了18.31%更高的任务成功率和2.3倍更快的策略合成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CodeLLMs在开放域具身环境中生成可执行代码策略时的两个根本限制：(i) 长提示词上重复预填充计算导致的延迟解码，(ii) 完全生成式解码导致的鲁棒性不足（API不匹配、缺少安全防护、控制逻辑不稳定）。

Method: FCGraft构建函数级验证代码骨架库及其提示级Transformer KV缓存；给定新任务时检索相关函数并嫁接其KV缓存，通过拼接（stitching）组合缓存函数片段为复合策略，以及修补（patching）局部调整必要代码区域以满足任务特定参数和约束，从而消除冗余预填充计算。

Result: 相比提示级缓存方法RAGCache，FCGraft实现了18.31%更高的任务成功率和2.3倍更快的策略合成速度。

Conclusion: FCGraft通过函数级缓存嫁接机制，有效降低了生成延迟并提高了策略鲁棒性，为开放域具身智能体的代码策略生成提供了高效且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Code-writing large language models (CodeLLMs) generate executable code policies for embodied agents by translating natural language goals and environmental constraints into structured control programs. However, policy generation in open-domain embodied environments suffers from two fundamental limitations: (i) delayed decoding caused by repetitive prefill computation over long prompts, and (ii) limited robustness due to fully generative decoding, which often produces API mismatches, missing safety guards, and unstable control logic. To address these limitations, we present FCGraft, a Functional Cache Grafting framework. FCGraft maintains a library of function-level validated code skeletons and their associated prompt-level Transformer key-value (KV) caches, and synthesizes new policies by retrieving relevant functions and grafting their KV caches when a new task is provided. Given retrieved function caches, FCGraft performs cache grafting via stitching, which composes cached function segments into a composite policy, and patching, which locally adapts only the necessary code regions to satisfy task-specific parameters and constraints with minimal additional decoding. By eliminating redundant prefill computation, this approach reduces generation latency, while reusing validated control structures improves robustness over prompt-level caching methods RAGCache, achieving 18.31% higher task success rate and 2.3x faster policy synthesis.

</details>


### [204] [Probabilistic, Resource-Aware, Asynchronous, Out-of-Order Choreographies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13520)
*Mako Bates,Steven Baldasty,Ernest Hyun,Christian Skalka,Joseph P. Near*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出AsInst，一种概率性、资源感知的语言，用于建模带乱序执行的异步编排语义，将其解释为时间贝叶斯网络，以分析运行时性能和故障恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Future的乱序编排实现能显著提升延迟和吞吐量，但其实际行为依赖于通信延迟、计算时间、故障和恢复等资源。现有形式化模型（如Ozone的O3）只描述可能的执行，无法解释这些执行的概率或耗时。

Method: 提出AsInst语言，将异步编排程序解释为时间贝叶斯网络，同时建模运行时产生的值及其可用时间；证明该语义正确对应Future风格的网络语义；可编码Ozone风格的选择-合并条件语句。

Result: 通过案例研究建模通信故障恢复并分析运行时性能。

Conclusion: AsInst为乱序编排的概率性和资源感知分析提供了形式化基础，能够解释执行的可能性和时间开销。

Abstract: Futures-based implementations of out-of-order choreographies can substantially improve latency and throughput, but their actual behavior depends on resources such as communication delay, computation time, failures, and recovery. Existing formal models such as Ozone's O3 describe which executions are possible, but do not directly explain how likely those executions are or how long they take. In this work we present AsInst, a probabilistic, resource-aware language for modeling the semantics of asynchronous choreographies with out-of-order execution. AsInst programs are interpreted as temporal Bayesian networks that model both the values produced at runtime and the times at which they become available. We prove that this central semantics correctly captures a corresponding futures-style network semantics. We also show that AsInst can encode Ozone-style select-and-merge conditionals, and we use case studies to model communication-failure recovery and analyze runtime performance.

</details>


### [205] [Specifying Hardware Communication as Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13659)
*Ernest Ng,Nikil Shyamsunder,Francis Pham,Adrian Sampson,Kevin Laeufer*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种DSL，允许用户将硬件通信协议描述为简洁的指令式程序，并利用同一份规范同时驱动设计和监控事务。工具可自动从波形中推断事务级轨迹，减少手动工作并避免不一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的驱动器和监视器为每个硬件协议分别实现，导致手动工作量大且容易出现不一致。

Method: 设计了一种DSL，让用户以指令式程序的形式指定硬件通信协议，同一规范同时用于驱动和监控；并开发了一个工具，根据波形自动推断事务级轨迹。

Result: 工具能根据给定的规范从波形中自动推断出事务级轨迹。

Conclusion: 该DSL方法有望减少手动编码工作并消除驱动与监视器之间的不一致性，计划在Wishbone和AXI-Stream等真实互连协议上评估。

Abstract: To test and debug hardware modules, it is common to write two programs: a driver, which translates high-level transactions into interactions on the module's input and output signals, and a monitor, which analyzes a signal-level execution trace and recognizes a transaction. These two programs are commonly implemented separately for each hardware protocol, but this separation entails manual effort and risks inconsistencies.
  We advocate an alternative approach. We present a DSL in which users specify hardware communication protocols as succinct imperative programs. Crucially, the same specification can be used to both drive designs and monitor transactions. We present the design of a tool, which given a specification in our DSL and a waveform, automatically infers a transaction-level trace consistent with the waveform. We discuss plans to evaluate our DSL on real-world interconnects such as Wishbone and AXI-Stream.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [206] [OpenRoundup: Multi-Table Data Wrangling Through Interactive Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12648)
*Stephen Kasica,Charles Berret,Tamara Munzner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OpenRoundup是一个开源、基于浏览器的系统，使数据记者能够在不编写代码的情况下，将多个表格整合为单个分析就绪的输出。该系统采用DuckDB-WASM的纯客户端架构，提供强数据隐私保障，并引入"急切表格整合"和"Stack/Pack声明式词汇"两个概念贡献。通过复制17个记者编程工作流程和4位专业记者部署研究，验证了其表达覆盖率和实用性，尤其适用于理解连接概念但缺乏编程技能的从业者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据记者在责任报道中经常需要整合多个独立来源的数据集，但现有交互式数据整理工具都以单个表格为工作单元，而非以表格集合为基本工作单元，缺乏专门支持多表整合的系统。

Method: 提出OpenRoundup系统，采用五面板协调界面实现schema优先、按需取值的范式，包含实时schema预览、数据质量警报和递归树图可视化操作树。架构上采用DuckDB-WASM纯客户端方案。引入两个核心概念：急切表格整合（在整理阶段早期通过交互式增量组装构建复合表）和声明式整合词汇（Stack和Pack两种操作）。

Result: 复制研究展示了系统对真实世界整合任务的表现覆盖率；部署研究确认了对理解连接概念但缺乏编程技能的从业者的实用性，并发现了一个未预期的附加价值：可用于数据新闻教育。

Conclusion: OpenRoundup有效填补了现有工具在多表整合方面的空白，为数据记者提供了无需编程的多表整合能力，同时保证了敏感数据的隐私安全，并具有教育领域的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Data journalists routinely integrate records across multiple independently published sources to support accountability reporting, yet no existing interactive wrangling tool treats the collection of tables -- rather than the single table -- as its primary unit of work. We present OpenRoundup, an open-source, browser-based system that enables data journalists to consolidate multiple tables into a single analysis-ready output without writing code. The interface comprises five coordinated panels that implement a schema-first, values-on-demand paradigm with live schema previews, ambient data quality alerts, and a recursive treemap visualization of the evolving operation tree. A client-only architecture powered by DuckDB-WASM runs in the browser, providing strong data privacy guarantees suited to sensitive journalism data. The system introduces two conceptual contributions: eager table consolidation, in which a composite table is assembled early in the wrangling phase via interactive, incremental assembly of multiple source tables; and a declarative vocabulary for table consolidation consisting of two operations, Stack and Pack. We evaluate the system through a replication study in which the authors reproduce 17 published journalist programming workflows using only the interface, and a deployment study with four professional data journalists. The replication study demonstrates expressive coverage of real-world consolidation tasks. The deployment study confirms utility for practitioners who understand joins conceptually but lack the programming skills to execute them, and surfaces an unanticipated secondary value for data journalism education.

</details>


### [207] [A Multiplexing Design Space: Theory, Method, and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12719)
*Yiwen Xing,Afrah Farea,Saiful Khan,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在受限应用场景下探索视觉复用设计空间的方法，并以机器学习工作流中多个2D标量场的可视化为例，通过三步设计法和预设计步骤，确定了相对最优的默认复用设计方案及用户可控的微小变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉复用现象在可视化设计中普遍存在但未被系统化探索，设计空间巨大，设计师通常无意识地使用复用设计。需要一种系统化的设计方法来约束设计空间，帮助发现有效的复用模式。

Method: 提出包含预设计步骤（领域基础分析与理论分析）和三个设计步骤的设计方法，结合领域专家的协同设计与评估活动，在多个2D标量场的可视化场景中探索视觉复用设计空间。

Result: 识别出相对最优的默认复用设计方案，以及领域专家可通过用户界面控制的微小变体需求。

Conclusion: 该设计方法能够系统化地探索受限应用场景下的视觉复用设计空间，为ML工作流中多个2D标量场的可视化提供了有效的设计指导。

Abstract: Many visualization designs feature phenomena referred to as ``visual multiplexing'', where multiple pieces of information associated with the same data point are conveyed simultaneously. Although visualization designers are able to bring such phenomena, often unconsciously, into their designs, the design space of visual multiplexing is huge, and it is uncommon to explore visual multiplexing systematically as design patterns. In this paper, we propose a design method for exploring a smaller design space constrained by an application. As an illustrative case study, we focus on machine learning (ML) workflows for developing ML models that approximate partial differential equations (PDEs). In these workflows, ML researchers need to analyze the inter-relationships among multiple 2D scalar fields frequently. Since superimposing one heatmap on top of another is not an effective design, we formulate three design steps to explore the design space of visual multiplexing in the context of multiple 2D scalar fields. Our design method also includes a pre-design step for domain grounding and theoretical analysis, and involves domain experts in both co-design and evaluation activities. The design process enables us to identify relatively optimal default multiplexing designs as well as the need for small variations that domain experts can control through a user interface.

</details>


### [208] [Exploring How Agent Voice Accents Shape Human-AI Collaboration in K-12 Group Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12805)
*Prerna Ravi,Carúmey Stevens,Ben Hurt,Brandon Hanks,Grace Lin,Emma Anderson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过33名教师的混合方法实验，研究GenAI语音代理的口音（英式、印度式、非裔美式）如何影响小组协作中的感知、信任和互动动态。英式口音代理被视为工具，而印度式和非裔美式口音代理更易被拟人化并作为同伴融入，口音塑造了参与者的心智模型和角色期望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作是21世纪教育的核心，但教师仍面临促进同伴互动的挑战。LLM对话同伴代理为调解小组工作带来新可能，但关于代理的声音特征（尤其是口音）如何塑造学习者的感知、信任和互动动态，尤其是小组环境下的研究尚属空白。

Method: 采用组间混合方法研究，33名教师参与实验，考察不同口音（英式、印度式、非裔美式）的GenAI语音代理对协作和代理感知的影响。通过问卷调查、小组互动分析和人工制品进行数据收集与分析。

Result: 口音显著影响参与者的心智模型及代理在小组互动中承担的角色。英式口音代理主要被视为工具，参与方式疏离且功利；印度式和非裔美式口音代理则更易被拟人化并作为同伴融入。这些角色期望随时间推移影响了信任、参与度和依赖程度。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了对GenAI社会语言学设计特征如何塑造CSCL中小组动态的理解，为设计具有文化包容性的AI学习伙伴提供了启示。

Abstract: Collaboration is widely recognized as a cornerstone of 21st-century education, yet teachers still encounter persistent challenges in fostering productive peer interaction. LLM conversational peer agents introduce new possibilities for mediating in-person group work, raising questions about how persona design, particularly their voice characteristics, shapes learners' perceptions, trust, and interactional dynamics. While prior work has examined agent accent effects in one-to-one settings, little is known about how these effects manifest in groups. We conducted a between-subjects mixed-methods study with 33 teachers examining how a GenAI voice agent with different accents (British, Indian, and African American) influenced collaboration and agent perception. Across surveys, group interaction analyses, and artifacts, we find that accent shaped participants' mental models and the roles the agent assumed in group interaction. The British-accented agent was largely treated as a tool and engaged in detached, utility-based ways, whereas Indian- and African American-accented agents were more readily anthropomorphized and integrated as peers. These role expectations influenced trust, engagement, and reliance over time. This work advances understanding of how GenAI's sociolinguistic design features shape group dynamics in CSCL, with implications for designing culturally inclusive AI partners in group learning.

</details>


### [209] [From Prompts to Preferences: An Open-Source Platform for Generative AI-Enhanced Conjoint Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12972)
*Philipp Brauner*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源、自托管的联合分析网络应用平台，使用生成式AI（大语言模型和文本到图像模型）创建集成刺激格式（文本场景描述和视觉刺激），并提供设置向导、AI辅助属性建议和实时数据分析，通过一个关于养老辅助机器人偏好的概念验证研究（N=55）展示了平台的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合分析工具要么昂贵，要么缺乏端到端调查基础设施，导致没有商业平台访问权限的研究人员面临显著障碍。

Method: 开发了一个开源自托管Web应用，集成了设置向导、AI辅助属性建议、基于LLM的文本场景生成、基于文本到图像模型的视觉刺激生成、实时数据分析以及包含刺激、生成提示和响应数据的完整导出包。

Result: 通过一个关于养老辅助机器人偏好的概念验证研究（N=55）展示了平台的有效性，使用了AI生成的视觉刺激。

Conclusion: AI辅助生成刺激可以拓宽HCI及相关领域的方法论库，但理论基础的构建仍是研究者的责任。

Abstract: Conjoint analysis is a widely used preference measurement method in marketing research, political science, healthcare, and human-computer interaction. Despite broad adoption, researchers without access to commercial platforms face significant barriers, as existing tools are either expensive or lack end-to-end survey infrastructure. This paper presents an open-source, self-hosted web application for designing, deploying, and analysing conjoint surveys. Beyond conventional tabular stimuli, the platform uses generative AI to produce integrated stimuli formats: textual scenario descriptions generated by a large language model, and visual stimuli by a text-to-image model. A researcher-defined base prompt is parameterised with the conjoint profile, and optional LLM-facing level annotations enrich the generation. A structured setup wizard, AI-assisted attribute suggestion, and live data analysis lower the technical barriers for researchers new to conjoint methodology. A full export bundle including all stimuli, their generating prompts, and response data facilitates transparency and reproducibility. The platform is demonstrated through a proof-of-concept study on care robot preferences for ambient assisted living (AAL, N=55) using AI-generated visual stimuli. The paper discusses the role of AI assistance in conjoint design, arguing that theoretical grounding must remain the researcher's responsibility, and outlining how genAI-generated stimuli can broaden the methodological repertoire for HCI and related fields.

</details>


### [210] [Mod-Guide: An LLM-based Content Moderation Feedback System to Address Insensitive Speech toward Indigenous Ethnic and Religious Minority Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13397)
*Dipto Das,Achhiya Sultana,Ankit Singh Chauhan,Saadia Binte Alam,Mohammad Shidujaman,Shion Guha,Sunandan Chakraborty,Syed Ishtiaque Ahmed*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了LLM-based内容审核系统对孟加拉国印度教和查克马社区文化不敏感言论的认知局限，并通过共同创建文化敏感语料库和检索增强生成（RAG）技术开发了Mod-Guide工具，显著提升了审核系统对少数群体观点的敏感性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言既可能造成边缘化也可能成为抵抗的工具，少数群体在网络上面临着不敏感甚至有害的言论。随着LLM越来越多地用于内容审核，这些系统能否识别文化不敏感言论（通过隐性抹除、歪曲或规范性框架而非公开敌意来边缘化少数群体的言论）成为关键问题。

Method: 与孟加拉国印度教和查克马社区成员共同创建文化敏感言论语料库，将社区叙事通过检索增强生成（RAG）整合到审核流程中，开发了Mod-Guide工具，并进行混合方法评估（包括少数群体和多数群体参与者）。

Result: RAG增强的审核回应在语境上更准确，且不同族群对其感知存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将恢复性正义和诠释学包容置于内容审核系统设计的核心，推进了人机交互、AI伦理和社会计算领域的研究。

Abstract: Language operates as a mechanism of both marginalization and resistance, especially for minority communities navigating insensitive and harmful speech online. As content moderation increasingly depends on large language models (LLMs), concerns arise about whether these systems can recognize culturally insensitive speech-language that disregards or marginalizes the cultural and religious perspectives of historically underrepresented communities, often through implicit erasure, misrepresentation, or normative framing, rather than overt hostility. Focusing on Bangladesh's Hindu and Chakma communities -- the country's largest religious and Indigenous ethnic minorities, respectively -- this paper investigates the epistemic limits of LLM-based moderation systems and explores methods for incorporating minority perspectives. We co-created a culturally grounded corpus of insensitive speech with community members and integrated their narratives into moderation pipelines using retrieval augmented generation (RAG). Our tool, Mod-Guide, improves LLM sensitivity to minority viewpoints by leveraging contextual cues derived from lived experience. Through mixed-method evaluations involving both minority and majority participants, we demonstrate that RAG-enhanced moderation responses are more contextually accurate and perceived differently across ethnic lines. This work advances research in human-computer interaction, AI ethics, and social computing by foregrounding restorative justice and hermeneutical inclusion in the design of content moderation systems.

</details>


### [211] [Ride, Track, and Recover: Pilot Randomized Trial of a Wearable Digital Self-Management Intervention During a Veteran Endurance-Cycling Program](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13529)
*Alan Ta,Nilsu Salgin,Caleb Armstrong,Kala Phillips Reindel,Farzan Sasangohar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在自然场景下探索了可穿戴设备结合数字干预工具对退伍军人创伤后应激障碍（PTSD）高唤醒症状的监测与管理效果。13名参与骑行活动的退伍军人被随机分为数字干预+体力活动组与仅体力活动组，另有7名居家监测对照。通过智能手表连续感测心率与加速度特征检测高唤醒事件，结合每周自我报告量表评估。结果显示数字干预组表现出稳定的高唤醒轨迹，且症状改善维持更好；ML检测精度与症状严重程度正相关。研究提示可穿戴检测与数字自我管理工具的结合可能支持高唤醒稳定化与症状改善，同时强调个性化与人本设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 退伍军人PTSD的高唤醒及共病焦虑、抑郁症状在临床环境外难以监测与管理，现有方法缺乏持续、客观的生理和行为监测手段，亟需探索可穿戴技术与数字干预在自然场景下的应用可行性。

Method: 13名退伍军人随机分为数字干预+体力活动组（n=7）与仅体力活动组（n=3），另设7名居家对照（n=4完成分析）。采用智能手表连续采集心率与加速度特征检测高唤醒事件并由参与者实时确认，同时收集每周自我报告（焦虑、抑郁、PTSD严重程度），使用广义加性混合模型（GAMM）刻画非线性轨迹。

Result: 数字干预组高唤醒轨迹呈结构化稳定，仅体力活动组后期出现升高；两组在耐力活动期间均有急性症状改善，但数字干预组整体维持效果更优；居家对照组症状逐渐下降。ML检测的感知精度个体间差异大，且与症状严重程度正相关。

Conclusion: 可穿戴检测与数字自我管理工具的结合可能有助于高唤醒稳定化与症状改善，但需重视个性化与人本设计以提升可穿戴心理健康系统的有效性。

Abstract: Post-traumatic stress disorder (PTSD) in veterans is characterized by persistent hyperarousal and comorbid anxiety and depressive symptoms that are difficult to monitor and manage outside clinical settings. Thirteen veterans participating in a Project Hero cycling event in Texas were randomized by computer-generated sequence in a naturalistic setting to two arms: (1) digital intervention plus physical activity, or (2) physical activity only, plus a third at-home monitoring control cohort consisting of 7 veterans selected from the broader Project Hero veteran community. Continuous smartwatch sensing combined heart rate and accelerometer features to detect hyperarousal events, which were confirmed in real time by participants. Weekly self-report measures of anxiety, depression, and PTSD severity were collected. Generalized additive mixed models characterized nonlinear trajectories over time. Baseline-normalized hyperarousal trajectories differed significantly across conditions, with the digital intervention group (n=7) showing structured stabilization compared to late-study escalation in the physical-only group (n=3). Both cycling groups exhibited acute symptom improvements during the endurance event; however, the digital intervention group demonstrated a higher overall maintenance of gains. The at-home control group (n=4) showed gradual symptom declines. Perceived precision of ML detections varied substantially across individuals and was positively associated with symptom severity, with higher-severity participants confirming a greater proportion of detected events. These results suggest that coupling wearable detection with digital self-management tools may support stabilization of hyperarousal and symptom improvement while emphasizing the importance of personalization and human-centered design in wearable mental health systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Modern analog computing for solving differential and matrix equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13179)
*Zhong Sun,Piergiulio Mannocci,Manuel Le Gallo,Abu Sebastian*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文综述了现代模拟计算领域，聚焦三大计算原语（求解微分方程、求解矩阵方程、执行矩阵-向量乘法），探讨了基于先进CMOS电路和电阻式存储器（尤其是电阻式存储阵列）的硬件实现，并讨论了精度、可扩展性、与存内计算的关系及计算复杂性等关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据密集型应用（如AI和科学计算）对计算能力的需求日益增长，推动了模拟计算的重新兴起。本文旨在统一视角审视现代模拟计算的演变，梳理其核心原语、硬件实现及最新进展。

Method: 识别三大核心计算原语（微分方程求解、矩阵方程求解、矩阵-向量乘法），分类考察不同硬件实现（分立元件、集成电路、电阻式存储器件），重点综述基于先进CMOS电路和电阻式存储阵列的模拟计算在求解微分和矩阵方程方面的进展。

Result: 电阻式存储阵列因其实现效率成为最有前景的方案；梳理了模拟计算在精度、可扩展性方面的挑战及潜在解决方案；探讨了与存内计算的关系以及模拟计算独特的计算复杂度问题。

Conclusion: 现代模拟计算是下一代计算前沿的关键推动者，但仍面临精度和可扩展性等挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: In recent years, driven by the computational demands of data-intensive applications such as artificial intelligence and scientific computing, analog computing has gained renewed interest. Given the diversity of computational tasks and recent advancements in analog CMOS circuits and resistive memory technologies, we refer to the evolving landscape as modern analog computing. In this context, we identify three core computational primitives: solving differential equations, solving matrix equations, and performing matrix-vector multiplications, and we explore the connections among them. We also examine various hardware implementations of these analog computing operators, including those built with discrete components, integrated circuits, and resistive memory devices. Among these, resistive memory arrays emerge as particularly promising due to their implementation efficiency. The paper then surveys recent progress in leveraging modern analog computing to solve differential and matrix equations using both advanced analog CMOS circuits and resistive memory arrays. Finally, we discuss the applications of these circuits, the precision and scalability issues and their potential solutions, the relationship with in-memory computing, and the unique computational complexity of analog computing. This paper provides a unified perspective on analog computing, highlighting its strengths, current developments, and challenges, and positioning it as a pivotal enabler of next-generation computational frontiers.

</details>
